<script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap@5.3.0/dist/js/bootstrap.bundle.min.js"></script>
<script>
    // Form validation
    function validateForm() {
        const form = document.querySelector('form');
        if (!form.checkValidity()) {
            form.classList.add('was-validated');
            return false;
        }
        return true;
    }

    // Load provinces on page load
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
        loadProvinces();

        // Add Bootstrap validation styling
        const form = document.querySelector('form');
        form.addEventListener('submit', function(event) {
            if (!form.checkValidity()) {
                event.preventDefault();
                event.stopPropagation();
            }
            form.classList.add('was-validated');
        });
    });

    function loadProvinces() {
        fetch('/api/locations/provinces')
            .then(response => response.json())
            .then(data => {
                const select = document.getElementById('province');
                select.innerHTML = '<option value="">Select province...</option>';
                data.forEach(item => {
                    select.innerHTML += `<option value="${item.id}">${item.name}</option>`;
                });
            })
            .catch(error => console.error('Error loading provinces:', error));
    }

    function loadDistricts() {
        const provinceId = document.getElementById('province').value;
        const districtSelect = document.getElementById('district');

        if (!provinceId) {
            districtSelect.disabled = true;
            resetSelects(['district', 'sector', 'cell', 'village']);
            return;
        }

        fetch(`/api/locations/districts/${provinceId}`)
            .then(response => response.json())
            .then(data => {
                districtSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">Select district...</option>';
                data.forEach(item => {
                    districtSelect.innerHTML += `<option value="${item.id}">${item.name}</option>`;
                });
                districtSelect.disabled = false;
                resetSelects(['sector', 'cell', 'village']);
            })
            .catch(error => console.error('Error loading districts:', error));
    }

    function loadSectors() {
        const districtId = document.getElementById('district').value;
        const sectorSelect = document.getElementById('sector');

        if (!districtId) {
            sectorSelect.disabled = true;
            resetSelects(['sector', 'cell', 'village']);
            return;
        }

        fetch(`/api/locations/sectors/${districtId}`)
            .then(response => response.json())
            .then(data => {
                sectorSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">Select sector...</option>';
                data.forEach(item => {
                    sectorSelect.innerHTML += `<option value="${item.id}">${item.name}</option>`;
                });
                sectorSelect.disabled = false;
                resetSelects(['cell', 'village']);
            })
            .catch(error => console.error('Error loading sectors:', error));
    }

    function loadCells() {
        const sectorId = document.getElementById('sector').value;
        const cellSelect = document.getElementById('cell');

        if (!sectorId) {
            cellSelect.disabled = true;
            resetSelects(['cell', 'village']);
            return;
        }

        fetch(`/api/locations/cells/${sectorId}`)
            .then(response => response.json())
            .then(data => {
                cellSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">Select cell...</option>';
                data.forEach(item => {
                    cellSelect.innerHTML += `<option value="${item.id}">${item.name}</option>`;
                });
                cellSelect.disabled = false;
                resetSelects(['village']);
            })
            .catch(error => console.error('Error loading cells:', error));
    }

    function loadVillages() {
        const cellId = document.getElementById('cell').value;
        const villageSelect = document.getElementById('village');

        if (!cellId) {
            villageSelect.disabled = true;
            return;
        }

        fetch(`/api/locations/villages/${cellId}`)
            .then(response => response.json())
            .then(data => {
                villageSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">Select village...</option>';
                data.forEach(item => {
                    villageSelect.innerHTML += `<option value="${item.id}">${item.name}</option>`;
                });
                villageSelect.disabled = false;
            })
            .catch(error => console.error('Error loading villages:', error));
    }

    function resetSelects(selectIds) {
        selectIds.forEach(id => {
            const select = document.getElementById(id);
            select.innerHTML = `<option value="">Select ${id}...</option>`;
            select.disabled = true;
        });
    }

    // Photo upload function
    function uploadPhoto() {
        const fileInput = document.getElementById('photoFile');
        const file = fileInput.files[0];

        if (!file) return;

        if (!file.type.startsWith('image/')) {
            alert('Please select an image file');
            return;
        }

        if (file.size > 5 * 1024 * 1024) {
            alert('File size must be less than 5MB');
            return;
        }

        const preview = document.getElementById('photoPreview');
        preview.innerHTML = '<div class="spinner-border text-primary" role="status"></div>';

        const formData = new FormData();
        formData.append('file', file);

        fetch('/api/upload/photo', {
            method: 'POST',
            body: formData
        })
        .then(response => response.json())
        .then(data => {
            if (data.success) {
                preview.innerHTML = `<img src="${data.url}" alt="Photo">`;
                document.getElementById('photoUrl').value = data.url;
            } else {
                alert('Upload failed: ' + data.message);
                preview.innerHTML = '<div class="placeholder"><i class="bi bi-camera" style="font-size: 3rem;"></i><p class="small mt-2">Upload failed</p></div>';
            }
        })
        .catch(error => {
            console.error('Error uploading photo:', error);
            alert('Error uploading photo');
            preview.innerHTML = '<div class="placeholder"><i class="bi bi-camera" style="font-size: 3rem;"></i><p class="small mt-2">Upload failed</p></div>';
        });
    }

    // PDF upload function
    function uploadPdf() {
        const fileInput = document.getElementById('pdfFile');
        const file = fileInput.files[0];

        if (!file) return;

        if (file.type !== 'application/pdf') {
            alert('Please select a PDF file');
            return;
        }

        if (file.size > 10 * 1024 * 1024) {
            alert('PDF file size must be less than 10MB');
            return;
        }

        const preview = document.getElementById('pdfPreview');
        preview.innerHTML = '<div class="spinner-border text-danger" role="status"></div>';
        preview.classList.add('show');

        const formData = new FormData();
        formData.append('file', file);

        fetch('/api/upload/pdf', {
            method: 'POST',
            body: formData
        })
        .then(response => response.json())
        .then(data => {
            if (data.success) {
                document.getElementById('pdfName').textContent = data.originalName;
                document.getElementById('pdfUrl').value = data.url;
                preview.innerHTML = `
                    <i class="bi bi-file-pdf text-danger" style="font-size: 2rem;"></i>
                    <strong id="pdfName" class="ms-2">${data.originalName}</strong>
                    <button type="button" class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-secondary ms-3" onclick="removePdf()">
                        <i class="bi bi-x-circle"></i> Remove
                    </button>
                `;
            } else {
                alert('Upload failed: ' + data.message);
                preview.classList.remove('show');
            }
        })
        .catch(error => {
            console.error('Error uploading PDF:', error);
            alert('Error uploading PDF');
            preview.classList.remove('show');
        });
    }

    function removePdf() {
        document.getElementById('pdfUrl').value = '';
        document.getElementById('pdfPreview').classList.remove('show');
        document.getElementById('pdfFile').value = '';
    }
</script>
</body>
</html>

<script>
    function toggleSidebar() {
        const sidebar = document.getElementById('sidebar');
        const overlay = document.getElementById('sidebarOverlay');
        sidebar.classList.toggle('active');
        overlay.classList.toggle('active');
    }

    function toggleSubmenu(menuId) {
        const submenu = document.getElementById('submenu-' + menuId);
        const menuHeader = event.currentTarget;

        document.querySelectorAll('.submenu').forEach(sub => {
            if (sub.id !== 'submenu-' + menuId) {
                sub.classList.remove('open');
            }
        });

        document.querySelectorAll('.menu-header').forEach(header => {
            if (header !== menuHeader) {
                header.classList.remove('active');
            }
        });

        submenu.classList.toggle('open');
        menuHeader.classList.toggle('active');
    }

    function showDashboard() {
        document.getElementById('dashboard-view').style.display = 'block';
        document.getElementById('iframe-view').style.display = 'none';

        document.querySelectorAll('.menu-header').forEach(header => {
            header.classList.remove('active');
        });

        document.querySelectorAll('.submenu').forEach(sub => {
            sub.classList.remove('open');
        });

        event.currentTarget.classList.add('active');

        if (window.innerWidth <= 768) {
            document.getElementById('sidebar').classList.remove('active');
            document.getElementById('sidebarOverlay').classList.remove('active');
        }
    }

    function loadContent(event) {
        document.getElementById('dashboard-view').style.display = 'none';
        document.getElementById('iframe-view').style.display = 'block';

        if (window.innerWidth <= 768) {
            document.getElementById('sidebar').classList.remove('active');
            document.getElementById('sidebarOverlay').classList.remove('active');
        }
    }

    window.addEventListener('resize', function() {
        if (window.innerWidth > 768) {
            document.getElementById('sidebar').classList.remove('active');
            document.getElementById('sidebarOverlay').classList.remove('active');
        }
    });
</script>
</body>

<!-- Password Validation Script -->
<script>
    document.querySelector('form').addEventListener('submit', function(e) {
        const password = document.getElementById('password').value;
        const confirmPassword = document.getElementById('confirmPassword').value;

        // Only validate if password is being changed
        if (password && password !== confirmPassword) {
            e.preventDefault();
            alert('Passwords do not match!');
            document.getElementById('confirmPassword').classList.add('is-invalid');
        }
    });

    // Clear validation on input
    document.getElementById('confirmPassword').addEventListener('input', function() {
        this.classList.remove('is-invalid');
    });
</script>

<script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap@5.3.0/dist/js/bootstrap.bundle.min.js"></script>
<script>
    function uploadPhoto() {
        const fileInput = document.getElementById('photoFile');
        const file = fileInput.files[0];

        if (!file) return;

        if (!file.type.startsWith('image/')) {
            alert('Please select an image file');
            return;
        }

        if (file.size > 5 * 1024 * 1024) {
            alert('File size must be less than 5MB');
            return;
        }

        const preview = document.getElementById('photoPreview');
        preview.style.display = 'flex';
        preview.innerHTML = '<div class="spinner-border text-primary" role="status"></div>';

        const formData = new FormData();
        formData.append('file', file);

        fetch('/api/upload/photo', {
            method: 'POST',
            body: formData
        })
        .then(response => response.json())
        .then(data => {
            if (data.success) {
                preview.innerHTML = `<img src="${data.url}" alt="New Photo">`;
                document.getElementById('photoUrl').value = data.url;
            } else {
                alert('Upload failed: ' + data.message);
                preview.style.display = 'none';
            }
        })
        .catch(error => {
            console.error('Error uploading photo:', error);
            alert('Error uploading photo');
            preview.style.display = 'none';
        });
    }

    function uploadPdf() {
        const fileInput = document.getElementById('pdfFile');
        const file = fileInput.files[0];

        if (!file) return;

        if (file.type !== 'application/pdf') {
            alert('Please select a PDF file');
            return;
        }

        if (file.size > 10 * 1024 * 1024) {
            alert('PDF file size must be less than 10MB');
            return;
        }

        const preview = document.getElementById('pdfPreview');
        preview.style.display = 'block';
        preview.innerHTML = '<div class="spinner-border text-danger" role="status"></div>';

        const formData = new FormData();
        formData.append('file', file);

        fetch('/api/upload/pdf', {
            method: 'POST',
            body: formData
        })
        .then(response => response.json())
        .then(data => {
            if (data.success) {
                document.getElementById('pdfUrl').value = data.url;
                preview.innerHTML = `
                    <i class="bi bi-file-pdf text-danger" style="font-size: 2rem;"></i>
                    <strong id="pdfName" class="ms-2">${data.originalName}</strong>
                    <button type="button" class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-secondary ms-3" onclick="removePdf()">
                        <i class="bi bi-x-circle"></i> Remove
                    </button>
                `;
            } else {
                alert('Upload failed: ' + data.message);
                preview.style.display = 'none';
            }
        })
        .catch(error => {
            console.error('Error uploading PDF:', error);
            alert('Error uploading PDF');
            preview.style.display = 'none';
        });
    }

    function removePdf() {
        document.getElementById('pdfUrl').value = '';
        document.getElementById('pdfPreview').style.display = 'none';
        document.getElementById('pdfFile').value = '';
    }

<script>
    // Simple search functionality
    document.getElementById('searchInput').addEventListener('keyup', function() {
        const searchValue = this.value.toLowerCase();
        const tableRows = document.querySelectorAll('#dataTable tbody tr');

        tableRows.forEach(row => {
            const text = row.textContent.toLowerCase();
            row.style.display = text.includes(searchValue) ? '' : 'none';
        });
    });
</script>

<script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap@5.3.0/dist/js/bootstrap.bundle.min.js"></script>
<script>
    // Form validation
    (function() {
        'use strict';
        const forms = document.querySelectorAll('.needs-validation');
        Array.from(forms).forEach(form => {
            form.addEventListener('submit', event => {
                if (!form.checkValidity()) {
                    event.preventDefault();
                    event.stopPropagation();
                }
                form.classList.add('was-validated');
            }, false);
        });
    })();

    function uploadPhoto() {
        const fileInput = document.getElementById('photoFile');
        const file = fileInput.files[0];

        if (!file) return;

        if (!file.type.startsWith('image/')) {
            alert('Please select an image file');
            return;
        }

        if (file.size > 5 * 1024 * 1024) {
            alert('File size must be less than 5MB');
            return;
        }

        const preview = document.getElementById('photoPreview');
        preview.innerHTML = '<div class="spinner-border text-primary" role="status"></div>';

        const formData = new FormData();
        formData.append('file', file);

        fetch('/api/upload/photo', {
            method: 'POST',
            body: formData
        })
        .then(response => response.json())
        .then(data => {
            if (data.success) {
                preview.innerHTML = `<img src="${data.url}" alt="Photo">`;
                document.getElementById('photoUrl').value = data.url;
            } else {
                alert('Upload failed: ' + data.message);
                preview.innerHTML = '<div class="placeholder"><i class="bi bi-camera" style="font-size: 3rem;"></i><p class="small mt-2">Upload failed</p></div>';
            }
        })
        .catch(error => {
            console.error('Error uploading photo:', error);
            alert('Error uploading photo');
            preview.innerHTML = '<div class="placeholder"><i class="bi bi-camera" style="font-size: 3rem;"></i><p class="small mt-2">Upload failed</p></div>';
        });
    }

    function uploadPdf() {
        const fileInput = document.getElementById('pdfFile');
        const file = fileInput.files[0];

        if (!file) return;

        if (file.type !== 'application/pdf') {
            alert('Please select a PDF file');
            return;
        }

        if (file.size > 10 * 1024 * 1024) {
            alert('PDF file size must be less than 10MB');
            return;
        }

        const preview = document.getElementById('pdfPreview');
        preview.innerHTML = '<div class="spinner-border text-danger" role="status"></div>';
        preview.classList.add('show');

        const formData = new FormData();
        formData.append('file', file);

        fetch('/api/upload/pdf', {
            method: 'POST',
            body: formData
        })
        .then(response => response.json())
        .then(data => {
            if (data.success) {
                document.getElementById('pdfName').textContent = data.originalName;
                document.getElementById('pdfUrl').value = data.url;
                preview.innerHTML = `
                    <i class="bi bi-file-pdf text-danger" style="font-size: 2rem;"></i>
                    <strong id="pdfName" class="ms-2">${data.originalName}</strong>
                    <button type="button" class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-secondary ms-3" onclick="removePdf()">
                        <i class="bi bi-x-circle"></i> Remove
                    </button>
                `;
            } else {
                alert('Upload failed: ' + data.message);
                preview.classList.remove('show');
            }
        })
        .catch(error => {
            console.error('Error uploading PDF:', error);
            alert('Error uploading PDF');
            preview.classList.remove('show');
        });
    }

    function removePdf() {
        document.getElementById('pdfUrl').value = '';
        document.getElementById('pdfPreview').classList.remove('show');
        document.getElementById('pdfFile').value = '';
    }
</script>
</body>

<script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap@5.3.0/dist/js/bootstrap.bundle.min.js"></script>
<script>
    function toggleSidebar() {
        const sidebar = document.getElementById('sidebar');
        const overlay = document.getElementById('sidebarOverlay');
        sidebar.classList.toggle('active');
        overlay.classList.toggle('active');
    }

    function toggleSubmenu(menuId) {
        const submenu = document.getElementById('submenu-' + menuId);
        const menuHeader = event.currentTarget;

        document.querySelectorAll('.submenu').forEach(sub => {
            if (sub.id !== 'submenu-' + menuId) {
                sub.classList.remove('open');
            }
        });

        document.querySelectorAll('.menu-header').forEach(header => {
            if (header !== menuHeader) {
                header.classList.remove('active');
            }
        });

        submenu.classList.toggle('open');
        menuHeader.classList.toggle('active');
    }

    function showDashboard() {
        document.getElementById('dashboard-view').style.display = 'block';
        document.getElementById('iframe-view').style.display = 'none';

        document.querySelectorAll('.menu-header').forEach(header => {
            header.classList.remove('active');
        });

        document.querySelectorAll('.submenu').forEach(sub => {
            sub.classList.remove('open');
        });

        event.currentTarget.classList.add('active');

        if (window.innerWidth <= 768) {
            document.getElementById('sidebar').classList.remove('active');
            document.getElementById('sidebarOverlay').classList.remove('active');
        }
    }

    function loadContent(event) {
        document.getElementById('dashboard-view').style.display = 'none';
        document.getElementById('iframe-view').style.display = 'block';

        if (window.innerWidth <= 768) {
            document.getElementById('sidebar').classList.remove('active');
            document.getElementById('sidebarOverlay').classList.remove('active');
        }
    }

    window.addEventListener('resize', function() {
        if (window.innerWidth > 768) {
            document.getElementById('sidebar').classList.remove('active');
            document.getElementById('sidebarOverlay').classList.remove('active');
        }
    });
</script>

<!-- Password Validation Script -->
<script>
    document.querySelector('form').addEventListener('submit', function(e) {
        const password = document.getElementById('password').value;
        const confirmPassword = document.getElementById('confirmPassword').value;

        if (password !== confirmPassword) {
            e.preventDefault();
            alert('Passwords do not match!');
            document.getElementById('confirmPassword').classList.add('is-invalid');
        }
    });
</script>

<script>
    function uploadPhoto() {
        const fileInput = document.getElementById('photoFile');
        const file = fileInput.files[0];

        if (!file) return;

        if (!file.type.startsWith('image/')) {
            alert('Please select an image file');
            return;
        }

        if (file.size > 5 * 1024 * 1024) {
            alert('File size must be less than 5MB');
            return;
        }

        const preview = document.getElementById('photoPreview');
        preview.style.display = 'flex';
        preview.innerHTML = '<div class="spinner-border text-primary" role="status"></div>';

        const formData = new FormData();
        formData.append('file', file);

        fetch('/api/upload/photo', {
            method: 'POST',
            body: formData
        })
        .then(response => response.json())
        .then(data => {
            if (data.success) {
                preview.innerHTML = `<img src="${data.url}" alt="New Photo">`;
                document.getElementById('photoUrl').value = data.url;
            } else {
                alert('Upload failed: ' + data.message);
                preview.style.display = 'none';
            }
        })
        .catch(error => {
            console.error('Error uploading photo:', error);
            alert('Error uploading photo');
            preview.style.display = 'none';
        });
    }

    function uploadPdf() {
        const fileInput = document.getElementById('pdfFile');
        const file = fileInput.files[0];

        if (!file) return;

        if (file.type !== 'application/pdf') {
            alert('Please select a PDF file');
            return;
        }

        if (file.size > 10 * 1024 * 1024) {
            alert('PDF file size must be less than 10MB');
            return;
        }

        const preview = document.getElementById('pdfPreview');
        preview.style.display = 'block';
        preview.innerHTML = '<div class="spinner-border text-danger" role="status"></div>';

        const formData = new FormData();
        formData.append('file', file);

        fetch('/api/upload/pdf', {
            method: 'POST',
            body: formData
        })
        .then(response => response.json())
        .then(data => {
            if (data.success) {
                document.getElementById('pdfUrl').value = data.url;
                preview.innerHTML = `
                    <i class="bi bi-file-pdf text-danger" style="font-size: 2rem;"></i>
                    <strong id="pdfName" class="ms-2">${data.originalName}</strong>
                    <button type="button" class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-secondary ms-3" onclick="removePdf()">
                        <i class="bi bi-x-circle"></i> Remove
                    </button>
                `;
            } else {
                alert('Upload failed: ' + data.message);
                preview.style.display = 'none';
            }
        })
        .catch(error => {
            console.error('Error uploading PDF:', error);
            alert('Error uploading PDF');
            preview.style.display = 'none';
        });
    }

    function removePdf() {
        document.getElementById('pdfUrl').value = '';
        document.getElementById('pdfPreview').style.display = 'none';
        document.getElementById('pdfFile').value = '';
    }
</script>
</body>
</html>

<script>
    // Simple search functionality
    document.getElementById('searchInput').addEventListener('keyup', function() {
        const searchValue = this.value.toLowerCase();
        const tableRows = document.querySelectorAll('#dataTable tbody tr');

        tableRows.forEach(row => {
            const text = row.textContent.toLowerCase();
            row.style.display = text.includes(searchValue) ? '' : 'none';
        });
    });
</script>

<script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap@5.3.0/dist/js/bootstrap.bundle.min.js"></script>
<script>
    // Form validation
    function validateForm() {
        const form = document.querySelector('form');
        if (!form.checkValidity()) {
            form.classList.add('was-validated');
            return false;
        }
        return true;
    }

    // Load provinces on page load
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
        loadProvinces();

        // Add Bootstrap validation styling
        const form = document.querySelector('form');
        form.addEventListener('submit', function(event) {
            if (!form.checkValidity()) {
                event.preventDefault();
                event.stopPropagation();
            }
            form.classList.add('was-validated');
        });
    });

    function loadProvinces() {
        fetch('/api/locations/provinces')
            .then(response => response.json())
            .then(data => {
                const select = document.getElementById('province');
                select.innerHTML = '<option value="">Select province...</option>';
                data.forEach(item => {
                    select.innerHTML += `<option value="${item.id}">${item.name}</option>`;
                });
            })
            .catch(error => console.error('Error loading provinces:', error));
    }

    function loadDistricts() {
        const provinceId = document.getElementById('province').value;
        const districtSelect = document.getElementById('district');

        if (!provinceId) {
            districtSelect.disabled = true;
            resetSelects(['district', 'sector', 'cell', 'village']);
            return;
        }

        fetch(`/api/locations/districts/${provinceId}`)
            .then(response => response.json())
            .then(data => {
                districtSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">Select district...</option>';
                data.forEach(item => {
                    districtSelect.innerHTML += `<option value="${item.id}">${item.name}</option>`;
                });
                districtSelect.disabled = false;
                resetSelects(['sector', 'cell', 'village']);
            })
            .catch(error => console.error('Error loading districts:', error));
    }

    function loadSectors() {
        const districtId = document.getElementById('district').value;
        const sectorSelect = document.getElementById('sector');

        if (!districtId) {
            sectorSelect.disabled = true;
            resetSelects(['sector', 'cell', 'village']);
            return;
        }

        fetch(`/api/locations/sectors/${districtId}`)
            .then(response => response.json())
            .then(data => {
                sectorSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">Select sector...</option>';
                data.forEach(item => {
                    sectorSelect.innerHTML += `<option value="${item.id}">${item.name}</option>`;
                });
                sectorSelect.disabled = false;
                resetSelects(['cell', 'village']);
            })
            .catch(error => console.error('Error loading sectors:', error));
    }

    function loadCells() {
        const sectorId = document.getElementById('sector').value;
        const cellSelect = document.getElementById('cell');

        if (!sectorId) {
            cellSelect.disabled = true;
            resetSelects(['cell', 'village']);
            return;
        }

        fetch(`/api/locations/cells/${sectorId}`)
            .then(response => response.json())
            .then(data => {
                cellSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">Select cell...</option>';
                data.forEach(item => {
                    cellSelect.innerHTML += `<option value="${item.id}">${item.name}</option>`;
                });
                cellSelect.disabled = false;
                resetSelects(['village']);
            })
            .catch(error => console.error('Error loading cells:', error));
    }

    function loadVillages() {
        const cellId = document.getElementById('cell').value;
        const villageSelect = document.getElementById('village');

        if (!cellId) {
            villageSelect.disabled = true;
            return;
        }

        fetch(`/api/locations/villages/${cellId}`)
            .then(response => response.json())
            .then(data => {
                villageSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">Select village...</option>';
                data.forEach(item => {
                    villageSelect.innerHTML += `<option value="${item.id}">${item.name}</option>`;
                });
                villageSelect.disabled = false;
            })
            .catch(error => console.error('Error loading villages:', error));
    }

    function resetSelects(selectIds) {
        selectIds.forEach(id => {
            const select = document.getElementById(id);
            select.innerHTML = `<option value="">Select ${id}...</option>`;
            select.disabled = true;
        });
    }

    // Photo upload function
    function uploadPhoto() {
        const fileInput = document.getElementById('photoFile');
        const file = fileInput.files[0];

        if (!file) return;

        if (!file.type.startsWith('image/')) {
            alert('Please select an image file');
            return;
        }

        if (file.size > 5 * 1024 * 1024) {
            alert('File size must be less than 5MB');
            return;
        }

        const preview = document.getElementById('photoPreview');
        preview.innerHTML = '<div class="spinner-border text-primary" role="status"></div>';

        const formData = new FormData();
        formData.append('file', file);

        fetch('/api/upload/photo', {
            method: 'POST',
            body: formData
        })
        .then(response => response.json())
        .then(data => {
            if (data.success) {
                preview.innerHTML = `<img src="${data.url}" alt="Photo">`;
                document.getElementById('photoUrl').value = data.url;
            } else {
                alert('Upload failed: ' + data.message);
                preview.innerHTML = '<div class="placeholder"><i class="bi bi-camera" style="font-size: 3rem;"></i><p class="small mt-2">Upload failed</p></div>';
            }
        })
        .catch(error => {
            console.error('Error uploading photo:', error);
            alert('Error uploading photo');
            preview.innerHTML = '<div class="placeholder"><i class="bi bi-camera" style="font-size: 3rem;"></i><p class="small mt-2">Upload failed</p></div>';
        });
    }

    // PDF upload function
    function uploadPdf() {
        const fileInput = document.getElementById('pdfFile');
        const file = fileInput.files[0];

        if (!file) return;

        if (file.type !== 'application/pdf') {
            alert('Please select a PDF file');
            return;
        }

        if (file.size > 10 * 1024 * 1024) {
            alert('PDF file size must be less than 10MB');
            return;
        }

        const preview = document.getElementById('pdfPreview');
        preview.innerHTML = '<div class="spinner-border text-danger" role="status"></div>';
        preview.classList.add('show');

        const formData = new FormData();
        formData.append('file', file);

        fetch('/api/upload/pdf', {
            method: 'POST',
            body: formData
        })
        .then(response => response.json())
        .then(data => {
            if (data.success) {
                document.getElementById('pdfName').textContent = data.originalName;
                document.getElementById('pdfUrl').value = data.url;
                preview.innerHTML = `
                    <i class="bi bi-file-pdf text-danger" style="font-size: 2rem;"></i>
                    <strong id="pdfName" class="ms-2">${data.originalName}</strong>
                    <button type="button" class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-secondary ms-3" onclick="removePdf()">
                        <i class="bi bi-x-circle"></i> Remove
                    </button>
                `;
            } else {
                alert('Upload failed: ' + data.message);
                preview.classList.remove('show');
            }
        })
        .catch(error => {
            console.error('Error uploading PDF:', error);
            alert('Error uploading PDF');
            preview.classList.remove('show');
        });
    }

    function removePdf() {
        document.getElementById('pdfUrl').value = '';
        document.getElementById('pdfPreview').classList.remove('show');
        document.getElementById('pdfFile').value = '';
    }
</script>
</body>

<script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap@5.3.0/dist/js/bootstrap.bundle.min.js"></script>
<script>
    function toggleSidebar() {
        const sidebar = document.getElementById('sidebar');
        const overlay = document.getElementById('sidebarOverlay');
        sidebar.classList.toggle('active');
        overlay.classList.toggle('active');
    }

    function toggleSubmenu(menuId) {
        const submenu = document.getElementById('submenu-' + menuId);
        const menuHeader = event.currentTarget;

        document.querySelectorAll('.submenu').forEach(sub => {
            if (sub.id !== 'submenu-' + menuId) {
                sub.classList.remove('open');
            }
        });

        document.querySelectorAll('.menu-header').forEach(header => {
            if (header !== menuHeader) {
                header.classList.remove('active');
            }
        });

        submenu.classList.toggle('open');
        menuHeader.classList.toggle('active');
    }

    function showDashboard() {
        document.getElementById('dashboard-view').style.display = 'block';
        document.getElementById('iframe-view').style.display = 'none';

        document.querySelectorAll('.menu-header').forEach(header => {
            header.classList.remove('active');
        });

        document.querySelectorAll('.submenu').forEach(sub => {
            sub.classList.remove('open');
        });

        event.currentTarget.classList.add('active');

        if (window.innerWidth <= 768) {
            document.getElementById('sidebar').classList.remove('active');
            document.getElementById('sidebarOverlay').classList.remove('active');
        }
    }

    function loadContent(event) {
        document.getElementById('dashboard-view').style.display = 'none';
        document.getElementById('iframe-view').style.display = 'block';

        if (window.innerWidth <= 768) {
            document.getElementById('sidebar').classList.remove('active');
            document.getElementById('sidebarOverlay').classList.remove('active');
        }
    }

    window.addEventListener('resize', function() {
        if (window.innerWidth > 768) {
            document.getElementById('sidebar').classList.remove('active');
            document.getElementById('sidebarOverlay').classList.remove('active');
        }
    });
</script>
</body>

        .edit-card {

<!-- Password Validation Script -->
<script>
    document.querySelector('form').addEventListener('submit', function(e) {
        const password = document.getElementById('password').value;
        const confirmPassword = document.getElementById('confirmPassword').value;

        // Only validate if password is being changed
        if (password && password !== confirmPassword) {
            e.preventDefault();
            alert('Passwords do not match!');
            document.getElementById('confirmPassword').classList.add('is-invalid');
        }
    });

    // Clear validation on input
    document.getElementById('confirmPassword').addEventListener('input', function() {
        this.classList.remove('is-invalid');
    });
</script>
